Facilitators that operate discovery services crawl and index\nthis. Also not mine — and, importantly, only a supplement: the ERC-8004 path\nworks even if every bazaar disappears.\n\n**Tertiary: the A2A agent card** at `/.well-known/agent-card.json`, referenced\nfrom the registration file's `services[]`. This is what an agent that already\nknows the domain (from a referral, an inbound link, a prior rating) uses to\nlearn my capabilities.\n\n### 2.1 What I must publish, and where\n\n| Artifact | Location | Purpose |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Registration file | `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-registration.json` | ERC-8004 `agentURI` target. Skills, price, endpoints, `supportedTrust`, back-pointing `registrations[]`. |\n| A2A agent card | `https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json` | A2A-native discovery; declares the x402 payment extension. |\n| OpenAPI document | `https://forecast.example.com/openapi.json` | Machine-callable request/response contract. |\n| Output JSON Schema | inline in the 402 `outputSchema` + bazaar extension | Lets a caller validate the response it paid for. |\n| Feedback mirrors | `https://forecast.example.com/feedback/{id}.json` | Optional convenience host for client-authored ratings (§6.2). |\n\n`/.well-known/agent-registration.json` doubles as ERC-8004's **domain-control\nproof**: the spec says a client MAY treat an endpoint domain as verified when\nthat file is reachable over HTTPS and its `registrations[]` entry matches the\nonchain `agentRegistry` + `agentId`. Because the chain points at the domain and\nthe domain points back at the chain, neither side can be forged alone — an\nimpostor can copy my JSON but cannot make the registry's `tokenURI` resolve to\ntheir host, and cannot mint a token that my DNS name endorses.\n\n### 2.2 What I must register, in order\n\n1. **DNS** — A/AAAA for `forecast.example.com`; CAA pinning the ACME CA.\n2. **TLS** — ACME/Let's Encrypt, auto-renewing. The entire discovery story rests\n   on HTTPS being the thing that binds the name to the content.\n3. **Publish the well-known files first.** `register()` writes a URI that clients\n   fetch immediately; a dangling `agentURI` is a failed trust check.\n4. **Onchain `register(agentURI, metadata[])`** on the Base IdentityRegistry →\n   returns `agentId`. Pay gas once, in ETH, from the operator wallet. This is the\n   *only* step in the whole system that requires the operator to hold ETH.\n5. **`setMetadata`** for the fields a client should be able to read without an\n   HTTP fetch — at minimum `payTo`, `x402.asset`, `x402.price`.\n6. **`setAgentWallet(agentId, payTo, deadline, signature)`** — binds the\n   payment-receiving address to the agent identity onchain, signed by that\n   wallet. Without this a squatter could publish a clone card with their own\n   `payTo` and harvest payments meant for me.\n7. **Backfill `registrations[]`** in the registration file with the `agentId`\n   returned in step 4, and redeploy. (Chicken-and-egg: the file must exist before\n   registration, and must be updated after it.)\n8. **Serve traffic.** The bazaar listing happens implicitly on the first 402.\n\n### 2.3 Registration file (served verbatim by `server.ts`)\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"type\": \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n  \"name\": \"forecast.example.com\",\n  \"description\": \"Point weather forecasts, 0-168h, $0.35 per call, USDC on Base.\",\n  \"services\": [\n    { \"name\": \"web\",  \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/\" },\n    { \"name\": \"A2A\",  \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/.well-known/agent-card.json\", \"version\": \"0.3.0\" }\n  ],\n  \"x402Support\": true,\n  \"active\": true,\n  \"registrations\": [\n    { \"agentId\": 4242, \"agentRegistry\": \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\" }\n  ],\n  \"supportedTrust\": [\"reputation\", \"crypto-economic\"]\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Judging trustworthiness without a human\n\nA client must decide *before* spending anything. Five checks, all machine-executable:\n\n1. **Identity is onchain, not self-asserted.** The `agentId` exists in a registry\n   I do not control, with a mint block that establishes age. A service registered\n   nine minutes ago is a different risk from one registered in 2025.\n2. **Bidirectional binding** (§2.1) proves the domain and the token are the same\n   operator.\n3. **Wallet binding.** `getAgentWallet(agentId) == payTo` in the 402 response. If\n   they differ, abort — someone is proxying my card with their own address.\n4. **Reputation aggregate.** `getSummary(agentId, [], \"\", \"\")` → `(count, value,\n   decimals)`. Free `eth_call`; no gas, no account. Filter by tag to ask narrower\n   questions (`getSummary(agentId, [], \"accuracy\", \"\")`).\n5. **Rater provenance.** `readAllFeedback` yields the rater addresses; each\n   feedback file carries `proofOfPayment.txHash`. Cross-check that hash against\n   USDC `Transfer` logs to `payTo`. A rating backed by a real $0.35 transfer costs\n   $0.35 to fake; a rating that isn't, costs nothing and should be discarded.\n\n**Sybil resistance is deliberately reader-side.** `giveFeedback` is permissionless\n— the contract only blocks the agent owner and its operators from rating\nthemselves. Anyone else can write anything. That is why `getSummary` and\n`readAllFeedback` take a `clientAddresses[]` filter: the honest aggregate is\nwhatever the *reader* computes over the raters *the reader* trusts. I cannot\ninflate my own score by curating the set, because I do not hold the filter. A\nclient with no opinion about who to trust should weight by proof-of-payment (check\n5) and by rater age.\n\n**Cold start, stated honestly.** At `count == 0` none of this proves quality —\nonly that I am a real, domain-bound, wallet-bound identity that has been around\nsince block N. That is a floor, not a recommendation. The intended bootstrap is\nthat $0.35 is small enough for a client to buy its own evidence: the price is\nitself the trust mechanism for the first call. `supportedTrust` therefore also\nadvertises `crypto-economic`, and §7 describes the validation path that upgrades\n\"they served me\" into \"they were right.\"\n\n---\n\n## 4. Payment — x402 `exact` on Base\n\n**Price.** $0.35 → USDC has 6 decimals → `maxAmountRequired: \"350000\"`, quoted as\na fixed atomic amount. Not a USD oracle lookup: USDC is the unit of account here,\nand a quote that moves between the 402 and the retry is a quote the client cannot\nsign.\n\n**The 402.** Unpaid requests get `402 Payment Required` with an `accepts[]` entry:\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"scheme\": \"exact\",\n  \"network\": \"base\",\n  \"maxAmountRequired\": \"350000\",\n  \"asset\": \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\",  // native USDC on Base\n  \"payTo\": \"0x…\",\n  \"resource\": \"https://forecast.example.com/v1/forecast?lat=…&lon=…\",\n  \"description\": \"Weather forecast, single point, up to 168h\",\n  \"mimeType\": \"application/json\",\n  \"outputSchema\": { /* … */ },\n  \"maxTimeoutSeconds\": 60,\n  \"extra\": { \"name\": \"USD Coin\", \"version\": \"2\" }\n}\n```\n\n`extra` is not decoration — it is the EIP-712 domain (`name`, `version`) of the\nUSDC contract, which the client needs to build a signature the token will accept.\nBase mainnet USDC uses `\"USD Coin\"` / `\"2\"`; Base Sepolia's uses `\"USDC\"` / `\"2\"`.\nGetting this wrong produces signatures that verify locally and revert onchain.\n\n### 4.1 Why \"USDC but no ETH\" is not a problem\n\nThis is the crux, and it is EIP-3009's entire reason for existing.\n\nThe client never sends a transaction. It signs a **`TransferWithAuthorization`**\nEIP-712 struct — `from`, `to`, `value`, `validAfter`, `validBefore`, `nonce` —\nwhich is just bytes. Signing costs nothing and touches no chain. It base64s that\ninto `X-PAYMENT` and sends an ordinary HTTPS request.\n\nThe **facilitator** submits `transferWithAuthorization` to the USDC contract and\npays the ETH gas from its own balance. USDC moves directly from the client to my\n`payTo`. The client's ETH balance is zero at the start and zero at the end, and\nit holds no relationship with the facilitator either.\n\nThree properties fall out of this, all of which the constraints demand:\n- No pre-funding: the authorization is drawn against the client's own balance at\n  settlement time. I never hold client money.\n- No accounts: `nonce` is a random 32 bytes, not a sequence number, so\n  authorizations need no per-payer state on either side.\n- No invoices: the 402 *is* the invoice, and it lives for `maxTimeoutSeconds`.\n\n### 4.2 Server-side verification rules\n\nThe server must **never** hand the facilitator the requirements the client echoed\nback. It reconstructs its own `PaymentRequirements` from local config and the\nactual request URL, and checks, before spending a facilitator call:\n\n- `scheme == \"exact\"`, `network == \"base\"`\n- `authorization.to == payTo` (case-insensitive)\n- `BigInt(authorization.value) >= 350000n`\n- `validBefore` is far enough ahead to survive settlement; `validAfter` is in the past\n\nThen `POST /verify`, do the work, `POST /settle`. Verify-before-work avoids\nserving unpaid calls; settle-after-work avoids charging for a call that errored.\nThe window between them is the residual risk: if settlement fails after the\nforecast is computed, the client gets a 402 and I have burned compute. At $0.35\nagainst a cached forecast that is the correct trade.\n\n**Idempotency.** The EIP-3009 nonce is the natural request key. The server caches\n`nonce → response` so a client retrying a dropped connection gets the same body\nback instead of a second settlement attempt (which would revert as a used\nauthorization anyway, but would return a confusing 402). The cache also closes\nthe replay window on the read path.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Onchain dependencies\n\n| Dependency | Address / id | Used for | If it breaks |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| Base L2 | `eip155:8453` | settlement, all registry state | Payments and ratings stall. Sequencer outage = service outage. |\n| USDC (native, Circle) | `0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913` | the money | Circle can freeze addresses; a frozen `payTo` stops all revenue. |\n| EIP-3009 on that contract | `transferWithAuthorization` | gasless payment | Not upgradeable away in practice, but it is a proxy — worth monitoring. |\n| ERC-8004 IdentityRegistry | `0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432` | discovery, identity, wallet binding | New clients cannot find me. Existing ones are unaffected. |\n| ERC-8004 ReputationRegistry | `0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63` | ratings | Trust signal goes stale; payments still work. |\n| ERC-8004 ValidationRegistry | canonical `0x8004Cb…` deployment (§7) | optional accuracy attestations | Optional path only. |\n| ERC-4337 EntryPoint + a USDC paymaster | e.g. Circle Paymaster on Base | lets an ETH-less client pay gas to *rate* me | Ratings become impossible for ETH-less clients — see §6.1. |\n\n**Note the registries are UUPS proxies** owned by the ERC-8004 deployer key. That\nis a real trust assumption, not a footnote: an upgrade could change registry\nsemantics under me. Mitigation is to snapshot `Registered` and `NewFeedback`\nevents locally so my identity and rating history remain provable from event logs\neven if the contract behaviour changes.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Offchain dependencies\n\n| Dependency | Role | Failure mode / mitigation |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| **x402 facilitator** (CDP `https://api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402`, or self-hosted) | `/verify`, `/settle`, pays gas | **The single most load-bearing offchain dependency.** If it is down, nobody can pay. Mitigate by running a fallback facilitator — it is just an RPC signer with an ETH balance and two endpoints. Config takes a list. |\n| **Base RPC provider** | reads registries, verifies proofs | Use two providers; disagreement is a signal, not a coin-flip. |\n| **DNS + ACME/TLS** | binds the name to the content | Expired cert breaks *discovery* (registration file unfetchable), not just transport. Alert well before renewal. |\n| **Weather data source** | the actual product | Rate limits and licence terms are mine to absorb. Cache aggressively; the same lat/lon/hour is one upstream call regardless of how many agents buy it. |\n| **IPFS pinning** (optional) | durable feedback files | Only used if a client asks me to mirror its rating. |\n| **Operator hot wallet** | one-time `register()`, `setAgentWallet` | Needs a little ETH once. Should not be the same key as `payTo`. |\n| **Receipt signing key** | signs receipts (§6.2) | Must be the agent wallet or a key published in the agent card, or receipts are unverifiable. |\n\nThe facilitator deserves emphasis: it sees every payment and could censor\nselectively. It cannot steal — the EIP-3009 authorization names `payTo` and\n`value`, and the signature covers both — but it can decline to broadcast. This is\nwhy facilitator choice is config, not a hardcoded constant.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Ratings\n\n### 7.1 The gas problem nobody mentions\n\n`giveFeedback` is an onchain write. The client that just paid me **has no ETH** —\nthat was the premise. So the naive \"client rates me onchain\" story is broken at\nstep one.\n\nTwo workable paths, both client-side, both of which I must support rather than\nassume away:\n\n- **ERC-4337 + a USDC paymaster** (Circle Paymaster is deployed on Base and takes\n  USDC for gas via an EIP-2612 permit). The client is a smart account; it submits\n  a UserOperation and pays gas in the same token it used to pay me. This is the\n  path I document in the agent card.\n- **A relayer.** Feedback is not authenticated to a specific submitter beyond\n  `msg.sender`, so a client without a smart account cannot delegate the write\n  without also delegating the identity that the rating is attributed to. This is\n  a genuine limitation of permissionless feedback: the rater must be able to\n  transact as itself.\n\nSo: an ETH-less client can *pay* me with no extra machinery, but must be a smart\naccount to *rate* me. Worth being explicit, because it means the rating loop has\na strictly higher client-side requirement than the payment loop.\n\n### 7.2 What the server does to enable it\n\nAfter settlement the server mints a **receipt** and returns `X-Receipt-Id`.\n`GET /v1/receipt/{id}` returns it, signed EIP-191 by the agent wallet:\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"receiptId\": \"…\",\n  \"agentId\": 4242,\n  \"agentRegistry\": \"eip155:8453:0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\",\n  \"endpoint\": \"https://forecast.example.com/v1/forecast\",\n  \"payer\": \"0x…\",\n  \"amount\": \"350000\",\n  \"asset\": \"eip155:8453/erc20:0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\",\n  \"txHash\": \"0x…\",          // the settlement transaction\n  \"requestHash\": \"0x…\",     // keccak of the canonical request\n  \"responseHash\": \"0x…\",    // keccak of the exact bytes served\n  \"servedAt\": \"2026-07-27T11:15:27Z\",\n  \"signature\": \"0x…\"        // EIP-191 over the above, by the agent wallet\n}\n```\n\nThe client turns that into an ERC-8004 feedback file — the spec's format, with\n`proofOfPayment` populated from `txHash` — hosts it (IPFS, its own domain, or\n`POST /v1/feedback` for me to mirror), and calls:\n\n```solidity\ngiveFeedback(\n  agentId,        // 4242\n  value,          // int128, e.g. 95\n  valueDecimals,  // 0\n  \"accuracy\",     // tag1\n  \"latency\",      // tag2\n  \"https://forecast.example.com/v1/forecast\",  // endpoint\n  feedbackURI,\n  feedbackHash    // keccak256 of the file bytes\n);\n```\n\n`responseHash` is the part that makes a rating *contestable* rather than merely\nloud. It commits me to the exact bytes I served, so if a client rates me 5/100\nfor a bad forecast, I can `appendResponse` with the evidence — and equally, I\ncannot later claim I served something I did not. Mutual, which is the point.\n\n**I deliberately do not gate rating on holding a receipt.** The registry is\npermissionless; a receipt-gated design would be theatre, since clients can rate\nme with or without one. The receipt's job is to make *honest* ratings verifiable\nby third parties, not to suppress dishonest ones. Suppression is the reader's job\n(§3), and the reader is better placed to do it.\n\nMirroring at `POST /v1/feedback` is a convenience with an obvious conflict of\ninterest — I could refuse to host a bad review. So the endpoint stores\ncontent-addressed and returns the hash, the client verifies the hash before\ncommitting it onchain, and the design assumes serious clients use IPFS. The\nonchain `feedbackHash` means my copy can be checked for tampering by anyone; it\ncannot be silently edited, only withheld — at which point the missing file is\nitself a signal.\n\n### 7.3 Validation (optional, and the strongest signal available)\n\nReputation says *\"it answered me.\"* For a forecast service the interesting claim\nis *\"it was right\"* — and unusually, that is objectively checkable after the fact.\nThe ValidationRegistry supports exactly this: an independent validator agent\ncompares my past forecasts against observed weather and calls\n`validationResponse(requestHash, response /* 0-100 */, responseURI, responseHash,\ntag)`. Because my receipts commit to `responseHash`, a validator can prove what I\npredicted without my cooperation. `supportedTrust` advertises this. It is the one\nsignal in this system that cannot be bought.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Security notes\n\n- **Reconstruct requirements server-side.** The client's echoed\n  `paymentRequirements` are untrusted input; a facilitator asked to verify\n  attacker-supplied requirements will happily confirm a $0.000001 payment.\n- **`payTo` is not the hot wallet.** Payments accrue to an address whose key is\n  not on the web server.\n- **Nonce cache is the idempotency and replay boundary** — bound its size and TTL\n  to `maxTimeoutSeconds` plus settlement latency.\n- **Receipts are signed but not secret.** They reveal the payer and amount, both\n  already public onchain.\n- **`resource` in the 402 must be the real, fully-qualified request URL**,\n  otherwise a client signing for one endpoint could be replayed against another.\n- **Rate-limit the free surface** (`/.well-known/*`, the 402 itself) independently\n  of the paid path; the 402 is unauthenticated and cheap to spam.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Honest limitations\n\n1. **Cold start is unsolved and unsolvable by design** (§3). Low price is the\n   mitigation, not a fix.\n2. **Feedback is permissionless**, so a raw average is meaningless. Every claim\n   this design makes about reputation depends on the *reader* filtering.\n3. **The rating loop requires a smart-account client** (§7.1), a strictly higher\n   bar than the payment loop.\n4. **The facilitator is a censorship chokepoint** — mitigated by making it\n   swappable, not eliminated.\n5. **ERC-8004 registries are upgradeable proxies** under a third-party owner.\n6. **ERC-8004 is a Draft ERC.** Addresses and signatures here are verified against\n   the July 2026 contracts repo; both can move before Final, and the registration\n   file `type` URI is version-pinned for that reason.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdSUovapZ9w4HDprr3bAL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the server."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":9452,"cache_read_input_tokens":57576,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":9452},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7771f28f-4ac8-40d8-8901-5e476f40630b","uuid":"da41d092-cddc-4aab-850a-245afef5ae1b","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:26:03.114Z","request_id":"req_011CdSUouJRqUDcFGvvfYMeJ"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdSUovapZ9w4HDprr3bAL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EFv8LMLAFAMzccMMrzipxJ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T111527Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/server.ts","content":"/**\n * forecast.example.com — service side.\n *\n * Weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $0.35/call, USDC on Base, settled\n * via x402. No accounts, no API keys, no humans. See design.md.\n *\n *   GET  /.well-known/agent-registration.json   ERC-8004 registration file\n *   GET  /.well-known/agent-card.json           A2A agent card\n *   GET  /openapi.json                          machine-readable API contract\n *   GET  /v1/forecast?lat&lon[&hours]           x402-gated product\n *   GET  /v1/receipt/{id}                       signed proof-of-service\n *   POST /v1/feedback                           optional mirror for ERC-8004 ratings\n *   GET  /feedback/{id}.json                    mirrored rating\n *   GET  /v1/reputation                         convenience read of onchain score\n *   GET  /health\n */\n\nimport { createServer, type IncomingMessage, type ServerResponse } from \"node:http\";\nimport { createHash } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport {\n  createPublicClient,\n  http as viemHttp,\n  keccak256,\n  toHex,\n  getAddress,\n  type Address,\n} from \"viem\";\nimport { base } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Config\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/** Native Circle USDC on Base. 6 decimals. Supports EIP-3009. */\nconst USDC_BASE: Address = \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\";\n/** EIP-712 domain of the token above — the client needs this to sign. */\nconst USDC_EIP712 = { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" } as const;\n\n/** ERC-8004 canonical deployments on Base mainnet. */\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY: Address = \"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\";\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY: Address = \"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\";\n\nconst CHAIN_ID = 8453;\nconst X402_NETWORK = \"base\";\nconst AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP = `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`;\n\n/** $0.35 at 6 decimals. Atomic units, as a string, per x402. */\nconst PRICE_ATOMIC = \"350000\";\n\nconst cfg = {\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT ?? 8402),\n  origin: process.env.ORIGIN ?? \"https://forecast.example.com\",\n  /** Address that receives payment. Not the web server's key. */\n  payTo: getAddress(process.env.PAY_TO ?? \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000001\"),\n  /** ERC-8004 agentId returned by register(). */\n  agentId: BigInt(process.env.AGENT_ID ?? \"0\"),\n  /** Facilitators, tried in order. Swappable on purpose: this is a chokepoint. */\n  facilitators: (process.env.FACILITATORS ?? \"https://api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402\")\n    .split(\",\")\n    .map((s) => s.trim())\n    .filter(Boolean),\n  facilitatorAuth: process.env.FACILITATOR_AUTH ?? \"\",\n  rpcUrl: process.env.BASE_RPC_URL ?? \"https://mainnet.base.org\",\n  /** Signs receipts. Should be the registered agent wallet (setAgentWallet). */\n  signerKey: process.env.RECEIPT_SIGNER_KEY as `0x${string}` | undefined,\n  upstream: process.env.WEATHER_UPSTREAM ?? \"\",\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: 60,\n};\n\nconst receiptSigner = cfg.signerKey ? privateKeyToAccount(cfg.signerKey) : undefined;\n\nconst chain = createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: viemHttp(cfg.rpcUrl) });\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// x402 types (specs/x402-specification-v1.md)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ntype PaymentRequirements = {\n  scheme: \"exact\";\n  network: string;\n  maxAmountRequired: string;\n  asset: string;\n  payTo: string;\n  resource: string;\n  description: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  outputSchema: unknown;\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;\n  extra: { name: string; version: string };\n};\n\ntype Authorization = {\n  from: string;\n  to: string;\n  value: string;\n  validAfter: string;\n  validBefore: string;\n  nonce: string;\n};\n\ntype PaymentPayload = {\n  x402Version: number;\n  scheme: string;\n  network: string;\n  payload: { signature: string; authorization: Authorization };\n};\n\ntype SettlementResponse = {\n  success: boolean;\n  errorReason?: string;\n  transaction: string;\n  network: string;\n  payer: string;\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Product schema — published in the 402 so a caller knows what it is buying\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nconst FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA = {\n  $schema: \"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema\",\n  type: \"object\",\n  required: [\"lat\", \"lon\", \"issuedAt\", \"periods\"],\n  properties: {\n    lat: { type: \"number\" },\n    lon: { type: \"number\" },\n    issuedAt: { type: \"string\", format: \"date-time\" },\n    source: { type: \"string\" },\n    periods: {\n      type: \"array\",\n      items: {\n        type: \"object\",\n        required: [\"time\", \"tempC\", \"windKph\", \"precipMm\", \"condition\"],\n        properties: {\n          time: { type: \"string\", format: \"date-time\" },\n          tempC: { type: \"number\" },\n          windKph: { type: \"number\" },\n          precipMm: { type: \"number\" },\n          condition: { type: \"string\" },\n        },\n      },\n    },\n  },\n} as const;\n\n/**\n * Requirements are rebuilt from local config on every request. The client's\n * echoed copy is untrusted input and is never forwarded to the facilitator.\n */\nfunction paymentRequirements(resourceUrl: string): PaymentRequirements {\n  return {\n    scheme: \"exact\",\n    network: X402_NETWORK,\n    maxAmountRequired: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n    asset: USDC_BASE,\n    payTo: cfg.payTo,\n    resource: resourceUrl,\n    description: \"Weather forecast for a single point, up to 168h ahead\",\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    outputSchema: FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA,\n    maxTimeoutSeconds: cfg.maxTimeoutSeconds,\n    extra: { ...USDC_EIP712 },\n  };\n}\n\n/** bazaar extension: how third-party discovery services index this endpoint. */\nconst BAZAAR_EXTENSION = {\n  info: {\n    input: {\n      type: \"http\",\n      method: \"GET\",\n      queryParams: { lat: \"52.52\", lon: \"13.405\", hours: \"24\" },\n    },\n    output: { type: \"json\", example: { lat: 52.52, lon: 13.405, periods: [] } },\n  },\n  schema: {\n    $schema: \"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema\",\n    type: \"object\",\n    properties: {\n      input: {\n        type: \"object\",\n        properties: {\n          type: { type: \"string\", const: \"http\" },\n          method: { type: \"string\", enum: [\"GET\"] },\n          queryParams: {\n            type: \"object\",\n            properties: {\n              lat: { type: \"string\" },\n              lon: { type: \"string\" },\n              hours: { type: \"string\" },\n            },\n            required: [\"lat\", \"lon\"],\n          },\n        },\n        required: [\"type\", \"method\"],\n        additionalProperties: false,\n      },\n      output: {\n        type: \"object\",\n        properties: { type: { type: \"string\" }, example: { type: \"object\" } },\n        required: [\"type\"],\n      },\n    },\n    required: [\"input\"],\n  },\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Facilitator client\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nasync function callFacilitator<T>(\n  path: \"/verify\" | \"/settle\",\n  paymentPayload: PaymentPayload,\n  reqs: PaymentRequirements,\n): Promise<T> {\n  const body = JSON.stringify({ x402Version: 1, paymentPayload, paymentRequirements: reqs });\n  const headers: Record<string, string> = { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" };\n  if (cfg.facilitatorAuth) headers.authorization = cfg.facilitatorAuth;\n\n  let lastErr: unknown;\n  for (const baseUrl of cfg.facilitators) {\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(baseUrl.replace(/\\/$/, \"\") + path, {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers,\n        body,\n        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(cfg.maxTimeoutSeconds * 1000),\n      });\n      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`facilitator ${baseUrl}${path} -> ${res.status}`);\n      return (await res.json()) as T;\n    } catch (err) {\n      lastErr = err;\n      console.warn(`[x402] facilitator ${baseUrl} failed:`, (err as Error).message);\n    }\n  }\n  throw lastErr ?? new Error(\"no facilitator configured\");\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Receipts + rating support\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ntype Receipt = {\n  receiptId: string;\n  agentId: string;\n  agentRegistry: string;\n  reputationRegistry: string;\n  endpoint: string;\n  payer: string;\n  amount: string;\n  asset: string;\n  txHash: string;\n  requestHash: string;\n  responseHash: string;\n  servedAt: string;\n  /** How to turn this receipt into an onchain rating. */\n  howToRate: {\n    contract: string;\n    function: string;\n    note: string;\n  };\n  signature?: string;\n};\n\nconst receipts = new Map<string, Receipt>();\n/** nonce -> served response. Idempotency + replay boundary. */\nconst settled = new Map<string, { body: string; receiptId: string; settlement: SettlementResponse }>();\n/** content-addressed mirrors of client-authored feedback files. */\nconst feedbackFiles = new Map<string, string>();\n\nconst sha256 = (s: string) => createHash(\"sha256\").update(s).digest(\"hex\");\n\nasync function buildReceipt(args: {\n  payer: string;\n  txHash: string;\n  requestUrl: string;\n  responseBody: string;\n}): Promise<Receipt> {\n  const receiptId = sha256(`${args.txHash}:${args.requestUrl}`).slice(0, 32);\n  const receipt: Receipt = {\n    receiptId,\n    agentId: cfg.agentId.toString(),\n    agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP,\n    reputationRegistry: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n    endpoint: `${cfg.origin}/v1/forecast`,\n    payer: args.payer,\n    amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n    asset: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}/erc20:${USDC_BASE}`,\n    txHash: args.txHash,\n    requestHash: keccak256(toHex(args.requestUrl)),\n    responseHash: keccak256(toHex(args.responseBody)),\n    servedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    howToRate: {\n      contract: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n      function:\n        \"giveFeedback(uint256 agentId,int128 value,uint8 valueDecimals,string tag1,string tag2,string endpoint,string feedbackURI,bytes32 feedbackHash)\",\n      note:\n        \"Build an ERC-8004 feedback file with proofOfPayment.txHash set to this receipt's txHash, \" +\n        \"host it (IPFS, your own domain, or POST /v1/feedback here), then call giveFeedback with \" +\n        \"feedbackHash = keccak256 of the file bytes. You need no ETH: pay gas in USDC via an \" +\n        \"ERC-4337 paymaster.\",\n    },\n  };\n\n  if (receiptSigner) {\n    // EIP-191 over the canonical receipt. Recoverable to the registered agent wallet.\n    const { signature: _drop, ...unsigned } = receipt;\n    receipt.signature = await receiptSigner.signMessage({\n      message: JSON.stringify(unsigned),\n    });\n  }\n  return receipt;\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// The actual product\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ntype Forecast = {\n  lat: number;\n  lon: number;\n  issuedAt: string;\n  source: string;\n  periods: Array<{\n    time: string;\n    tempC: number;\n    windKph: number;\n    precipMm: number;\n    condition: string;\n  }>;\n};\n\nconst forecastCache = new Map<string, { at: number; value: Forecast }>();\nconst FORECAST_TTL_MS = 10 * 60_000;\n\nasync function getForecast(lat: number, lon: number, hours: number): Promise<Forecast> {\n  const key = `${lat.toFixed(3)}:${lon.toFixed(3)}:${hours}`;\n  const hit = forecastCache.get(key);\n  if (hit && Date.now() - hit.at < FORECAST_TTL_MS) return hit.value;\n\n  let value: Forecast;\n  if (cfg.upstream) {\n    const url = new URL(cfg.upstream);\n    url.searchParams.set(\"latitude\", String(lat));\n    url.searchParams.set(\"longitude\", String(lon));\n    url.searchParams.set(\"hourly\", \"temperature_2m,wind_speed_10m,precipitation\");\n    url.searchParams.set(\"forecast_hours\", String(hours));\n    const res = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000) });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`upstream ${res.status}`);\n    const raw = (await res.json()) as {\n      hourly: {\n        time: string[];\n        temperature_2m: number[];\n        wind_speed_10m: number[];\n        precipitation: number[];\n      };\n    };\n    value = {\n      lat,\n      lon,\n      issuedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      source: new URL(cfg.upstream).host,\n      periods: raw.hourly.time.map((t, i) => ({\n        time: t,\n        tempC: raw.hourly.temperature_2m[i],\n        windKph: raw.hourly.wind_speed_10m[i],\n        precipMm: raw.hourly.precipitation[i],\n        condition: raw.hourly.precipitation[i] > 0.2 ? \"rain\" : \"clear\",\n      })),\n    };\n  } else {\n    // Deterministic stand-in so the payment path is exercisable without an upstream key.\n    const issued = new Date();\n    value = {\n      lat,\n      lon,\n      issuedAt: issued.toISOString(),\n      source: \"synthetic\",\n      periods: Array.from({ length: hours }, (_, i) => {\n        const t = new Date(issued.getTime() + i * 3600_000);\n        const seasonal = Math.sin((t.getUTCHours() / 24) * 2 * Math.PI);\n        return {\n          time: t.toISOString(),\n          tempC: Number((12 + 8 * seasonal - Math.abs(lat) / 6).toFixed(1)),\n          windKph: Number((10 + 5 * Math.cos(i / 3)).toFixed(1)),\n          precipMm: i % 7 === 0 ? 0.4 : 0,\n          condition: i % 7 === 0 ? \"rain\" : \"clear\",\n        };\n      }),\n    };\n  }\n\n  forecastCache.set(key, { at: Date.now(), value });\n  return value;\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Published documents\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/** ERC-8004 registration file. Target of tokenURI(agentId); also the domain-control proof. */\nfunction registrationFile() {\n  return {\n    type: \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description:\n      \"Point weather forecasts, 0-168h ahead. $0.35 per call in USDC on Base via x402. \" +\n      \"No accounts, no API keys.\",\n    image: `${cfg.origin}/icon.png`,\n    services: [\n      { name: \"web\", endpoint: `${cfg.origin}/` },\n      { name: \"A2A\", endpoint: `${cfg.origin}/.well-known/agent-card.json`, version: \"0.3.0\" },\n    ],\n    x402Support: true,\n    active: true,\n    // Back-pointer completing the bidirectional domain <-> chain binding.\n    registrations: [{ agentId: Number(cfg.agentId), agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP }],\n    supportedTrust: [\"reputation\", \"crypto-economic\"],\n  };\n}\n\n/** A2A agent card, referenced from the registration file's services[]. */\nfunction agentCard() {\n  return {\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Weather forecasts for autonomous agents, priced per call in USDC on Base.\",\n    url: `${cfg.origin}/v1/forecast`,\n    preferredTransport: \"HTTP+JSON\",\n    version: \"1.0.0\",\n    provider: { organization: \"forecast.example.com\", url: cfg.origin },\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, pushNotifications: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point weather forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly forecast for a lat/lon, up to 168 hours ahead.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"meteorology\"],\n        examples: [\"lat=52.52&lon=13.405&hours=24\"],\n        inputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n        outputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n      },\n    ],\n    securitySchemes: {},\n    security: [],\n    // How to pay, and how to rate afterwards — both machine-readable.\n    extensions: [\n      {\n        uri: \"https://x402.org/extensions/payment\",\n        required: true,\n        params: {\n          x402Version: 1,\n          scheme: \"exact\",\n          network: X402_NETWORK,\n          asset: USDC_BASE,\n          amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n          decimals: 6,\n          payTo: cfg.payTo,\n          extra: USDC_EIP712,\n        },\n      },\n      {\n        uri: \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004\",\n        required: false,\n        params: {\n          agentId: Number(cfg.agentId),\n          identityRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP,\n          reputationRegistry: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n          receiptEndpoint: `${cfg.origin}/v1/receipt/{receiptId}`,\n          gaslessRatingHint:\n            \"giveFeedback is an onchain write; an ETH-less caller should submit it as an \" +\n            \"ERC-4337 UserOperation with a USDC paymaster on Base.\",\n        },\n      },\n    ],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction openApiDocument() {\n  return {\n    openapi: \"3.1.0\",\n    info: {\n      title: \"forecast.example.com\",\n      version: \"1.0.0\",\n      description: \"Weather forecasts, $0.35/call, USDC on Base via x402.\",\n    },\n    servers: [{ url: cfg.origin }],\n    paths: {\n      \"/v1/forecast\": {\n        get: {\n          summary: \"Point weather forecast\",\n          parameters: [\n            { name: \"lat\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\" } },\n            { name: \"lon\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\" } },\n            {\n              name: \"hours\",\n              in: \"query\",\n              required: false,\n              schema: { type: \"integer\", minimum: 1, maximum: 168, default: 24 },\n            },\n            {\n              name: \"X-PAYMENT\",\n              in: \"header\",\n              required: false,\n              description: \"base64(x402 PaymentPayload). Omit to receive a 402 quote.\",\n              schema: { type: \"string\" },\n            },\n          ],\n          responses: {\n            \"200\": {\n              description: \"Forecast. X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE carries the settlement receipt.\",\n              content: { \"application/json\": { schema: FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA } },\n            },\n            \"402\": { description: \"Payment required. Body carries x402 accepts[].\" },\n          },\n        },\n      },\n      \"/v1/receipt/{receiptId}\": {\n        get: {\n          summary: \"Signed proof of service, for use when rating via ERC-8004\",\n          parameters: [\n            { name: \"receiptId\", in: \"path\", required: true, schema: { type: \"string\" } },\n          ],\n          responses: { \"200\": { description: \"Receipt\" }, \"404\": { description: \"Unknown\" } },\n        },\n      },\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// HTTP plumbing\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction send(res: ServerResponse, status: number, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) {\n  const payload = typeof body === \"string\" ? body : JSON.stringify(body, null, 2);\n  res.writeHead(status, {\n    \"content-type\": \"application/json; charset=utf-8\",\n    \"cache-control\": \"no-store\",\n    \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n    \"access-control-expose-headers\": \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE, X-Receipt-Id\",\n    ...headers,\n  });\n  res.end(payload);\n}\n\nfunction decodePayment(header: string): PaymentPayload {\n  return JSON.parse(Buffer.from(header, \"base64\").toString(\"utf8\")) as PaymentPayload;\n}\n\nfunction encodeSettlement(s: SettlementResponse): string {\n  return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(s), \"utf8\").toString(\"base64\");\n}\n\n/**\n * Local checks before spending a facilitator round-trip. The facilitator repeats\n * all of these, but doing them here keeps obviously-bad payments cheap and makes\n * the failure reasons legible to the caller.\n */\nfunction precheck(p: PaymentPayload, reqs: PaymentRequirements): string | null {\n  if (p?.scheme !== \"exact\") return `unsupported scheme: ${p?.scheme}`;\n  if (p?.network !== reqs.network) return `wrong network: ${p?.network}`;\n  const a = p?.payload?.authorization;\n  if (!a || !p.payload.signature) return \"malformed payload\";\n  if (a.to?.toLowerCase() !== reqs.payTo.toLowerCase()) return \"authorization.to != payTo\";\n  let value: bigint;\n  try {\n    value = BigInt(a.value);\n  } catch {\n    return \"authorization.value is not an integer\";\n  }\n  if (value < BigInt(reqs.maxAmountRequired)) return \"insufficient_amount\";\n  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);\n  if (Number(a.validBefore) <= now + 5) return \"authorization expires too soon\";\n  if (Number(a.validAfter) > now) return \"authorization not yet valid\";\n  return null;\n}\n\nasync function handleForecast(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse, url: URL) {\n  const lat = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lat\"));\n  const lon = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lon\"));\n  const hours = Math.min(168, Math.max(1, Number(url.searchParams.get(\"hours\") ?? 24)));\n\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon) || Math.abs(lat) > 90 || Math.abs(lon) > 180) {\n    return send(res, 400, { error: \"lat and lon are required and must be valid coordinates\" });\n  }\n\n  const resourceUrl = `${cfg.origin}${url.pathname}${url.search}`;\n  const reqs = paymentRequirements(resourceUrl);\n  const header = req.headers[\"x-payment\"];\n\n  // No payment attached -> quote. This response is the entire onboarding flow:\n  // it tells a stranger the price, the token, the chain, the EIP-712 domain it\n  // must sign over, and the schema of what it will receive.\n  if (!header || typeof header !== \"string\") {\n    return send(res, 402, {\n      x402Version: 1,\n      error: \"X-PAYMENT header is required\",\n      accepts: [reqs],\n      extensions: { bazaar: BAZAAR_EXTENSION },\n    });\n  }\n\n  let payment: PaymentPayload;\n  try {\n    payment = decodePayment(header);\n  } catch {\n    return send(res, 402, {\n      x402Version: 1,\n      error: \"X-PAYMENT is not valid base64-encoded JSON\",\n      accepts: [reqs],\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Idempotency: a retried request with the same EIP-3009 nonce replays the\n  // stored response rather than attempting a second settlement.\n  const nonce = payment.payload?.authorization?.nonce;\n  const prior = nonce ? settled.get(nonce) : undefined;\n  if (prior) {\n    return send(res, 200, prior.body, {\n      \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE\": encodeSettlement(prior.settlement),\n      \"X-Receipt-Id\": prior.receiptId,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const bad = precheck(payment, reqs);\n  if (bad) {\n    return send(res, 402, { x402Version: 1, error: bad, accepts: [reqs] });\n  }\n\n  // 1. Verify before doing any work.\n  let verification: { isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: string };\n  try {\n    verification = await callFacilitator(\"/verify\", payment, reqs);\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[x402] verify failed:\", err);\n    return send(res, 503, { x402Version: 1, error: \"payment verification unavailable\" });\n  }\n  if (!verification.isValid) {\n    return send(res, 402, {\n      x402Version: 1,\n      error: verification.invalidReason ?? \"payment_invalid\",\n      accepts: [reqs],\n    });\n  }\n\n  // 2. Do the work. If this throws, we have not settled, so nobody is charged.\n  let forecast: Forecast;\n  try {\n    forecast = await getForecast(lat, lon, hours);\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[forecast] upstream failed:\", err);\n    return send(res, 502, { error: \"forecast upstream unavailable; you were not charged\" });\n  }\n\n  // 3. Settle. The facilitator broadcasts transferWithAuthorization and pays the\n  //    gas, which is what lets a caller holding only USDC pay us.\n  let settlement: SettlementResponse;\n  try {\n    settlement = await callFacilitator(\"/settle\", payment, reqs);\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[x402] settle failed:\", err);\n    return send(res, 402, { x402Version: 1, error: \"settlement_failed\", accepts: [reqs] });\n  }\n  if (!settlement.success) {\n    return send(\n      res,\n      402,\n      { x402Version: 1, error: settlement.errorReason ?? \"settlement_failed\", accepts: [reqs] },\n      { \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE\": encodeSettlement(settlement) },\n    );\n  }\n\n  const body = JSON.stringify(forecast, null, 2);\n  const receipt = await buildReceipt({\n    payer: settlement.payer ?? verification.payer ?? payment.payload.authorization.from,\n    txHash: settlement.transaction,\n    requestUrl: resourceUrl,\n    responseBody: body,\n  });\n  receipts.set(receipt.receiptId, receipt);\n  if (nonce) settled.set(nonce, { body, receiptId: receipt.receiptId, settlement });\n\n  return send(res, 200, body, {\n    \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE\": encodeSettlement(settlement),\n    \"X-Receipt-Id\": receipt.receiptId,\n  });\n}\n\n/** Mirror a client-authored ERC-8004 feedback file. Content-addressed. */\nasync function handleFeedbackMirror(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse) {\n  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];\n  let size = 0;\n  for await (const chunk of req) {\n    size += chunk.length;\n    if (size > 64 * 1024) return send(res, 413, { error: \"feedback file too large\" });\n    chunks.push(chunk as Buffer);\n  }\n  const raw = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString(\"utf8\");\n  try {\n    JSON.parse(raw);\n  } catch {\n    return send(res, 400, { error: \"body must be a JSON feedback file\" });\n  }\n\n  const id = sha256(raw).slice(0, 32);\n  feedbackFiles.set(id, raw);\n  return send(res, 201, {\n    feedbackURI: `${cfg.origin}/feedback/${id}.json`,\n    // The value to pass as giveFeedback's feedbackHash. Verify it yourself\n    // before committing: we host this, so you should not trust us to host it\n    // honestly. IPFS is the censorship-resistant option.\n    feedbackHash: keccak256(toHex(raw)),\n    note: \"Verify feedbackHash against your own bytes before calling giveFeedback.\",\n  });\n}\n\n/** Convenience read of the onchain score. Clients should prefer their own RPC. */\nasync function handleReputation(res: ServerResponse) {\n  const abi = [\n    {\n      type: \"function\",\n      name: \"getSummary\",\n      stateMutability: \"view\",\n      inputs: [\n        { name: \"agentId\", type: \"uint256\" },\n        { name: \"clientAddresses\", type: \"address[]\" },\n        { name: \"tag1\", type: \"string\" },\n        { name: \"tag2\", type: \"string\" },\n      ],\n      outputs: [\n        { name: \"count\", type: \"uint64\" },\n        { name: \"summaryValue\", type: \"int128\" },\n        { name: \"summaryValueDecimals\", type: \"uint8\" },\n      ],\n    },\n  ] as const;\n\n  try {\n    const [count, value, decimals] = await chain.readContract({\n      address: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n      abi,\n      functionName: \"getSummary\",\n      args: [cfg.agentId, [], \"\", \"\"],\n    });\n    return send(res, 200, {\n      agentId: cfg.agentId.toString(),\n      reputationRegistry: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n      count: count.toString(),\n      summaryValue: value.toString(),\n      summaryValueDecimals: decimals,\n      disclaimer:\n        \"Self-reported convenience read of a public contract. Read it yourself, and filter \" +\n        \"by clientAddresses you trust — giveFeedback is permissionless.\",\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    return send(res, 503, { error: `could not read registry: ${(err as Error).message}` });\n  }\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Router\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nconst server = createServer(async (req, res) => {\n  const url = new URL(req.url ?? \"/\", cfg.origin);\n  const path = url.pathname;\n\n  try {\n    if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") {\n      return send(res, 204, \"\", { \"access-control-allow-headers\": \"X-PAYMENT, content-type\" });\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"GET\") {\n      switch (path) {\n        case \"/.well-known/agent-registration.json\":\n          // Cacheable: this is the file strangers fetch to verify the binding.\n          return send(res, 200, registrationFile(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n        case \"/.well-known/agent-card.json\":\n          return send(res, 200, agentCard(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n        case \"/openapi.json\":\n          return send(res, 200, openApiDocument(), { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=300\" });\n        case \"/v1/forecast\":\n          return await handleForecast(req, res, url);\n        case \"/v1/reputation\":\n          return await handleReputation(res);\n        case \"/health\":\n          return send(res, 200, { ok: true, agentId: cfg.agentId.toString() });\n      }\n\n      const receiptMatch = /^\\/v1\\/receipt\\/([A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64})$/.exec(path);\n      if (receiptMatch) {\n        const receipt = receipts.get(receiptMatch[1]);\n        return receipt\n          ? send(res, 200, receipt)\n          : send(res, 404, { error: \"unknown receipt\" });\n      }\n\n      const feedbackMatch = /^\\/feedback\\/([A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64})\\.json$/.exec(path);\n      if (feedbackMatch) {\n        const file = feedbackFiles.get(feedbackMatch[1]);\n        return file\n          ? send(res, 200, file, { \"cache-control\": \"public, max-age=31536000, immutable\" })\n          : send(res, 404, { error: \"unknown feedback file\" });\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (req.method === \"POST\" && path === \"/v1/feedback\") {\n      return await handleFeedbackMirror(req, res);\n    }\n\n    return send(res, 404, { error: \"not found\", discover: `${cfg.origin}/openapi.json` });\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[server] unhandled:\", err);\n    return send(res, 500, { error: \"internal error\" });\n  }\n});\n\nserver.listen(cfg.port, () => {\n  console.log(`forecast.example.com listening on :${cfg.port}`);\n  console.log(`  origin      ${cfg.origin}`);\n  console.log(`  agentId     ${cfg.agentId}  @ ${AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP}`);\n  console.log(`  payTo       ${cfg.payTo}`);\n  console.log(`  price       ${PRICE_ATOMIC} atomic USDC ($0.35) on ${X402_NETWORK}`);\n  console.log(`  facilitator ${cfg.facilitators.join(\", \")}`);\n  if (!receiptSigner) console.warn(\"  WARNING: RECEIPT_SIGNER_KEY unset — receipts unsigned\");\n  if (cfg.agentId === 0n) console.warn(\"  WARNING: AGENT_ID unset — register() on the IdentityRegistry first\");\n});\n\nexport { server, paymentRequirements, precheck, registrationFile, agentCard };\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":9452,"cache_read_input_tokens":57576,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":9452},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7771f28f-4ac8-40d8-8901-5e476f40630b","uuid":"a4d17fe4-d96c-43b8-ae3d-c30c02cfa96b","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:27:58.577Z","request_id":"req_011CdSUouJRqUDcFGvvfYMeJ"}
{"type":"rate_limit_event","rate_limit_info":{"status":"allowed","resetsAt":1785154200,"rateLimitType":"five_hour","overageStatus":"allowed","overageResetsAt":1785151800,"isUsingOverage":false},"uuid":"c227af47-5687-4f37-b1f3-20a64bc75770","session_id":"7771f28f-4ac8-40d8-8901-5e476f40630b"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EFv8LMLAFAMzccMMrzipxJ","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T111527Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/server.ts (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7771f28f-4ac8-40d8-8901-5e476f40630b","uuid":"c61a801d-df8f-4490-9a84-851e44915e51","timestamp":"2026-07-27T11:27:58.614Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/standards-goal-001/2026-07-27T111527Z-claude-no-skill-2/workspace/server.ts","content":"/**\n * forecast.example.com — service side.\n *\n * Weather forecasts for autonomous agents. $0.35/call, USDC on Base, settled\n * via x402. No accounts, no API keys, no humans. See design.md.\n *\n *   GET  /.well-known/agent-registration.json   ERC-8004 registration file\n *   GET  /.well-known/agent-card.json           A2A agent card\n *   GET  /openapi.json                          machine-readable API contract\n *   GET  /v1/forecast?lat&lon[&hours]           x402-gated product\n *   GET  /v1/receipt/{id}                       signed proof-of-service\n *   POST /v1/feedback                           optional mirror for ERC-8004 ratings\n *   GET  /feedback/{id}.json                    mirrored rating\n *   GET  /v1/reputation                         convenience read of onchain score\n *   GET  /health\n */\n\nimport { createServer, type IncomingMessage, type ServerResponse } from \"node:http\";\nimport { createHash } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport {\n  createPublicClient,\n  http as viemHttp,\n  keccak256,\n  toHex,\n  getAddress,\n  type Address,\n} from \"viem\";\nimport { base } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { privateKeyToAccount } from \"viem/accounts\";\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Config\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/** Native Circle USDC on Base. 6 decimals. Supports EIP-3009. */\nconst USDC_BASE: Address = \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\";\n/** EIP-712 domain of the token above — the client needs this to sign. */\nconst USDC_EIP712 = { name: \"USD Coin\", version: \"2\" } as const;\n\n/** ERC-8004 canonical deployments on Base mainnet. */\nconst IDENTITY_REGISTRY: Address = \"0x8004A169FB4a3325136EB29fA0ceB6D2e539a432\";\nconst REPUTATION_REGISTRY: Address = \"0x8004BAa17C55a88189AE136b182e5fdA19dE9b63\";\n\nconst CHAIN_ID = 8453;\nconst X402_NETWORK = \"base\";\nconst AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP = `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${IDENTITY_REGISTRY}`;\n\n/** $0.35 at 6 decimals. Atomic units, as a string, per x402. */\nconst PRICE_ATOMIC = \"350000\";\n\nconst cfg = {\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT ?? 8402),\n  origin: process.env.ORIGIN ?? \"https://forecast.example.com\",\n  /** Address that receives payment. Not the web server's key. */\n  payTo: getAddress(process.env.PAY_TO ?? \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000001\"),\n  /** ERC-8004 agentId returned by register(). */\n  agentId: BigInt(process.env.AGENT_ID ?? \"0\"),\n  /** Facilitators, tried in order. Swappable on purpose: this is a chokepoint. */\n  facilitators: (process.env.FACILITATORS ?? \"https://api.cdp.coinbase.com/platform/v2/x402\")\n    .split(\",\")\n    .map((s) => s.trim())\n    .filter(Boolean),\n  facilitatorAuth: process.env.FACILITATOR_AUTH ?? \"\",\n  rpcUrl: process.env.BASE_RPC_URL ?? \"https://mainnet.base.org\",\n  /** Signs receipts. Should be the registered agent wallet (setAgentWallet). */\n  signerKey: process.env.RECEIPT_SIGNER_KEY as `0x${string}` | undefined,\n  upstream: process.env.WEATHER_UPSTREAM ?? \"\",\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: 60,\n};\n\nconst receiptSigner = cfg.signerKey ? privateKeyToAccount(cfg.signerKey) : undefined;\n\nconst chain = createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: viemHttp(cfg.rpcUrl) });\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// x402 types (specs/x402-specification-v1.md)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ntype PaymentRequirements = {\n  scheme: \"exact\";\n  network: string;\n  maxAmountRequired: string;\n  asset: string;\n  payTo: string;\n  resource: string;\n  description: string;\n  mimeType: string;\n  outputSchema: unknown;\n  maxTimeoutSeconds: number;\n  extra: { name: string; version: string };\n};\n\ntype Authorization = {\n  from: string;\n  to: string;\n  value: string;\n  validAfter: string;\n  validBefore: string;\n  nonce: string;\n};\n\ntype PaymentPayload = {\n  x402Version: number;\n  scheme: string;\n  network: string;\n  payload: { signature: string; authorization: Authorization };\n};\n\ntype SettlementResponse = {\n  success: boolean;\n  errorReason?: string;\n  transaction: string;\n  network: string;\n  payer: string;\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Product schema — published in the 402 so a caller knows what it is buying\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nconst FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA = {\n  $schema: \"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema\",\n  type: \"object\",\n  required: [\"lat\", \"lon\", \"issuedAt\", \"periods\"],\n  properties: {\n    lat: { type: \"number\" },\n    lon: { type: \"number\" },\n    issuedAt: { type: \"string\", format: \"date-time\" },\n    source: { type: \"string\" },\n    periods: {\n      type: \"array\",\n      items: {\n        type: \"object\",\n        required: [\"time\", \"tempC\", \"windKph\", \"precipMm\", \"condition\"],\n        properties: {\n          time: { type: \"string\", format: \"date-time\" },\n          tempC: { type: \"number\" },\n          windKph: { type: \"number\" },\n          precipMm: { type: \"number\" },\n          condition: { type: \"string\" },\n        },\n      },\n    },\n  },\n} as const;\n\n/**\n * Requirements are rebuilt from local config on every request. The client's\n * echoed copy is untrusted input and is never forwarded to the facilitator.\n */\nfunction paymentRequirements(resourceUrl: string): PaymentRequirements {\n  return {\n    scheme: \"exact\",\n    network: X402_NETWORK,\n    maxAmountRequired: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n    asset: USDC_BASE,\n    payTo: cfg.payTo,\n    resource: resourceUrl,\n    description: \"Weather forecast for a single point, up to 168h ahead\",\n    mimeType: \"application/json\",\n    outputSchema: FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA,\n    maxTimeoutSeconds: cfg.maxTimeoutSeconds,\n    extra: { ...USDC_EIP712 },\n  };\n}\n\n/** bazaar extension: how third-party discovery services index this endpoint. */\nconst BAZAAR_EXTENSION = {\n  info: {\n    input: {\n      type: \"http\",\n      method: \"GET\",\n      queryParams: { lat: \"52.52\", lon: \"13.405\", hours: \"24\" },\n    },\n    output: { type: \"json\", example: { lat: 52.52, lon: 13.405, periods: [] } },\n  },\n  schema: {\n    $schema: \"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema\",\n    type: \"object\",\n    properties: {\n      input: {\n        type: \"object\",\n        properties: {\n          type: { type: \"string\", const: \"http\" },\n          method: { type: \"string\", enum: [\"GET\"] },\n          queryParams: {\n            type: \"object\",\n            properties: {\n              lat: { type: \"string\" },\n              lon: { type: \"string\" },\n              hours: { type: \"string\" },\n            },\n            required: [\"lat\", \"lon\"],\n          },\n        },\n        required: [\"type\", \"method\"],\n        additionalProperties: false,\n      },\n      output: {\n        type: \"object\",\n        properties: { type: { type: \"string\" }, example: { type: \"object\" } },\n        required: [\"type\"],\n      },\n    },\n    required: [\"input\"],\n  },\n};\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Facilitator client\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nasync function callFacilitator<T>(\n  path: \"/verify\" | \"/settle\",\n  paymentPayload: PaymentPayload,\n  reqs: PaymentRequirements,\n): Promise<T> {\n  const body = JSON.stringify({ x402Version: 1, paymentPayload, paymentRequirements: reqs });\n  const headers: Record<string, string> = { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" };\n  if (cfg.facilitatorAuth) headers.authorization = cfg.facilitatorAuth;\n\n  let lastErr: unknown;\n  for (const baseUrl of cfg.facilitators) {\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(baseUrl.replace(/\\/$/, \"\") + path, {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers,\n        body,\n        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(cfg.maxTimeoutSeconds * 1000),\n      });\n      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`facilitator ${baseUrl}${path} -> ${res.status}`);\n      return (await res.json()) as T;\n    } catch (err) {\n      lastErr = err;\n      console.warn(`[x402] facilitator ${baseUrl} failed:`, (err as Error).message);\n    }\n  }\n  throw lastErr ?? new Error(\"no facilitator configured\");\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Receipts + rating support\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ntype Receipt = {\n  receiptId: string;\n  agentId: string;\n  agentRegistry: string;\n  reputationRegistry: string;\n  endpoint: string;\n  payer: string;\n  amount: string;\n  asset: string;\n  txHash: string;\n  requestHash: string;\n  responseHash: string;\n  servedAt: string;\n  /** How to turn this receipt into an onchain rating. */\n  howToRate: {\n    contract: string;\n    function: string;\n    note: string;\n  };\n  signature?: string;\n};\n\nconst receipts = new Map<string, Receipt>();\n/** nonce -> served response. Idempotency + replay boundary. */\nconst settled = new Map<string, { body: string; receiptId: string; settlement: SettlementResponse }>();\n/** content-addressed mirrors of client-authored feedback files. */\nconst feedbackFiles = new Map<string, string>();\n\nconst sha256 = (s: string) => createHash(\"sha256\").update(s).digest(\"hex\");\n\nasync function buildReceipt(args: {\n  payer: string;\n  txHash: string;\n  requestUrl: string;\n  responseBody: string;\n}): Promise<Receipt> {\n  const receiptId = sha256(`${args.txHash}:${args.requestUrl}`).slice(0, 32);\n  const receipt: Receipt = {\n    receiptId,\n    agentId: cfg.agentId.toString(),\n    agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP,\n    reputationRegistry: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n    endpoint: `${cfg.origin}/v1/forecast`,\n    payer: args.payer,\n    amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n    asset: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}/erc20:${USDC_BASE}`,\n    txHash: args.txHash,\n    requestHash: keccak256(toHex(args.requestUrl)),\n    responseHash: keccak256(toHex(args.responseBody)),\n    servedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    howToRate: {\n      contract: REPUTATION_REGISTRY,\n      function:\n        \"giveFeedback(uint256 agentId,int128 value,uint8 valueDecimals,string tag1,string tag2,string endpoint,string feedbackURI,bytes32 feedbackHash)\",\n      note:\n        \"Build an ERC-8004 feedback file with proofOfPayment.txHash set to this receipt's txHash, \" +\n        \"host it (IPFS, your own domain, or POST /v1/feedback here), then call giveFeedback with \" +\n        \"feedbackHash = keccak256 of the file bytes. You need no ETH: pay gas in USDC via an \" +\n        \"ERC-4337 paymaster.\",\n    },\n  };\n\n  if (receiptSigner) {\n    // EIP-191 over the canonical receipt. Recoverable to the registered agent wallet.\n    const { signature: _drop, ...unsigned } = receipt;\n    receipt.signature = await receiptSigner.signMessage({\n      message: JSON.stringify(unsigned),\n    });\n  }\n  return receipt;\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// The actual product\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ntype Forecast = {\n  lat: number;\n  lon: number;\n  issuedAt: string;\n  source: string;\n  periods: Array<{\n    time: string;\n    tempC: number;\n    windKph: number;\n    precipMm: number;\n    condition: string;\n  }>;\n};\n\nconst forecastCache = new Map<string, { at: number; value: Forecast }>();\nconst FORECAST_TTL_MS = 10 * 60_000;\n\nasync function getForecast(lat: number, lon: number, hours: number): Promise<Forecast> {\n  const key = `${lat.toFixed(3)}:${lon.toFixed(3)}:${hours}`;\n  const hit = forecastCache.get(key);\n  if (hit && Date.now() - hit.at < FORECAST_TTL_MS) return hit.value;\n\n  let value: Forecast;\n  if (cfg.upstream) {\n    const url = new URL(cfg.upstream);\n    url.searchParams.set(\"latitude\", String(lat));\n    url.searchParams.set(\"longitude\", String(lon));\n    url.searchParams.set(\"hourly\", \"temperature_2m,wind_speed_10m,precipitation\");\n    url.searchParams.set(\"forecast_hours\", String(hours));\n    const res = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000) });\n    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`upstream ${res.status}`);\n    const raw = (await res.json()) as {\n      hourly: {\n        time: string[];\n        temperature_2m: number[];\n        wind_speed_10m: number[];\n        precipitation: number[];\n      };\n    };\n    value = {\n      lat,\n      lon,\n      issuedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      source: new URL(cfg.upstream).host,\n      periods: raw.hourly.time.map((t, i) => ({\n        time: t,\n        tempC: raw.hourly.temperature_2m[i],\n        windKph: raw.hourly.wind_speed_10m[i],\n        precipMm: raw.hourly.precipitation[i],\n        condition: raw.hourly.precipitation[i] > 0.2 ? \"rain\" : \"clear\",\n      })),\n    };\n  } else {\n    // Deterministic stand-in so the payment path is exercisable without an upstream key.\n    const issued = new Date();\n    value = {\n      lat,\n      lon,\n      issuedAt: issued.toISOString(),\n      source: \"synthetic\",\n      periods: Array.from({ length: hours }, (_, i) => {\n        const t = new Date(issued.getTime() + i * 3600_000);\n        const seasonal = Math.sin((t.getUTCHours() / 24) * 2 * Math.PI);\n        return {\n          time: t.toISOString(),\n          tempC: Number((12 + 8 * seasonal - Math.abs(lat) / 6).toFixed(1)),\n          windKph: Number((10 + 5 * Math.cos(i / 3)).toFixed(1)),\n          precipMm: i % 7 === 0 ? 0.4 : 0,\n          condition: i % 7 === 0 ? \"rain\" : \"clear\",\n        };\n      }),\n    };\n  }\n\n  forecastCache.set(key, { at: Date.now(), value });\n  return value;\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Published documents\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/** ERC-8004 registration file. Target of tokenURI(agentId); also the domain-control proof. */\nfunction registrationFile() {\n  return {\n    type: \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004#registration-v1\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description:\n      \"Point weather forecasts, 0-168h ahead. $0.35 per call in USDC on Base via x402. \" +\n      \"No accounts, no API keys.\",\n    image: `${cfg.origin}/icon.png`,\n    services: [\n      { name: \"web\", endpoint: `${cfg.origin}/` },\n      { name: \"A2A\", endpoint: `${cfg.origin}/.well-known/agent-card.json`, version: \"0.3.0\" },\n    ],\n    x402Support: true,\n    active: true,\n    // Back-pointer completing the bidirectional domain <-> chain binding.\n    registrations: [{ agentId: Number(cfg.agentId), agentRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP }],\n    supportedTrust: [\"reputation\", \"crypto-economic\"],\n  };\n}\n\n/** A2A agent card, referenced from the registration file's services[]. */\nfunction agentCard() {\n  return {\n    protocolVersion: \"0.3.0\",\n    name: \"forecast.example.com\",\n    description: \"Weather forecasts for autonomous agents, priced per call in USDC on Base.\",\n    url: `${cfg.origin}/v1/forecast`,\n    preferredTransport: \"HTTP+JSON\",\n    version: \"1.0.0\",\n    provider: { organization: \"forecast.example.com\", url: cfg.origin },\n    capabilities: { streaming: false, pushNotifications: false },\n    defaultInputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    defaultOutputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n    skills: [\n      {\n        id: \"point-forecast\",\n        name: \"Point weather forecast\",\n        description: \"Hourly forecast for a lat/lon, up to 168 hours ahead.\",\n        tags: [\"weather\", \"forecast\", \"meteorology\"],\n        examples: [\"lat=52.52&lon=13.405&hours=24\"],\n        inputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n        outputModes: [\"application/json\"],\n      },\n    ],\n    securitySchemes: {},\n    security: [],\n    // How to pay, and how to rate afterwards — both machine-readable.\n    extensions: [\n      {\n        uri: \"https://x402.org/extensions/payment\",\n        required: true,\n        params: {\n          x402Version: 1,\n          scheme: \"exact\",\n          network: X402_NETWORK,\n          asset: USDC_BASE,\n          amount: PRICE_ATOMIC,\n          decimals: 6,\n          payTo: cfg.payTo,\n          extra: USDC_EIP712,\n        },\n      },\n      {\n        uri: \"https://eips.ethereum.org/EIPS/eip-8004\",\n        required: false,\n        params: {\n          agentId: Number(cfg.agentId),\n          identityRegistry: AGENT_REGISTRY_CAIP,\n          reputationRegistry: `eip155:${CHAIN_ID}:${REPUTATION_REGISTRY}`,\n          receiptEndpoint: `${cfg.origin}/v1/receipt/{receiptId}`,\n          gaslessRatingHint:\n            \"giveFeedback is an onchain write; an ETH-less caller should submit it as an \" +\n            \"ERC-4337 UserOperation with a USDC paymaster on Base.\",\n        },\n      },\n    ],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction openApiDocument() {\n  return {\n    openapi: \"3.1.0\",\n    info: {\n      title: \"forecast.example.com\",\n      version: \"1.0.0\",\n      description: \"Weather forecasts, $0.35/call, USDC on Base via x402.\",\n    },\n    servers: [{ url: cfg.origin }],\n    paths: {\n      \"/v1/forecast\": {\n        get: {\n          summary: \"Point weather forecast\",\n          parameters: [\n            { name: \"lat\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\" } },\n            { name: \"lon\", in: \"query\", required: true, schema: { type: \"number\" } },\n            {\n              name: \"hours\",\n              in: \"query\",\n              required: false,\n              schema: { type: \"integer\", minimum: 1, maximum: 168, default: 24 },\n            },\n            {\n              name: \"X-PAYMENT\",\n              in: \"header\",\n              required: false,\n              description: \"base64(x402 PaymentPayload). Omit to receive a 402 quote.\",\n              schema: { type: \"string\" },\n            },\n          ],\n          responses: {\n            \"200\": {\n              description: \"Forecast. X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE carries the settlement receipt.\",\n              content: { \"application/json\": { schema: FORECAST_OUTPUT_SCHEMA } },\n            },\n            \"402\": { description: \"Payment required. Body carries x402 accepts[].\" },\n          },\n        },\n      },\n      \"/v1/receipt/{receiptId}\": {\n        get: {\n          summary: \"Signed proof of service, for use when rating via ERC-8004\",\n          parameters: [\n            { name: \"receiptId\", in: \"path\", required: true, schema: { type: \"string\" } },\n          ],\n          responses: { \"200\": { description: \"Receipt\" }, \"404\": { description: \"Unknown\" } },\n        },\n      },\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// HTTP plumbing\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nfunction send(res: ServerResponse, status: number, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) {\n  const payload = typeof body === \"string\" ? body : JSON.stringify(body, null, 2);\n  res.writeHead(status, {\n    \"content-type\": \"application/json; charset=utf-8\",\n    \"cache-control\": \"no-store\",\n    \"access-control-allow-origin\": \"*\",\n    \"access-control-expose-headers\": \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE, X-Receipt-Id\",\n    ...headers,\n  });\n  res.end(payload);\n}\n\nfunction decodePayment(header: string): PaymentPayload {\n  return JSON.parse(Buffer.from(header, \"base64\").toString(\"utf8\")) as PaymentPayload;\n}\n\nfunction encodeSettlement(s: SettlementResponse): string {\n  return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(s), \"utf8\").toString(\"base64\");\n}\n\n/**\n * Local checks before spending a facilitator round-trip. The facilitator repeats\n * all of these, but doing them here keeps obviously-bad payments cheap and makes\n * the failure reasons legible to the caller.\n */\nfunction precheck(p: PaymentPayload, reqs: PaymentRequirements): string | null {\n  if (p?.scheme !== \"exact\") return `unsupported scheme: ${p?.scheme}`;\n  if (p?.network !== reqs.network) return `wrong network: ${p?.network}`;\n  const a = p?.payload?.authorization;\n  if (!a || !p.payload.signature) return \"malformed payload\";\n  if (a.to?.toLowerCase() !== reqs.payTo.toLowerCase()) return \"authorization.to != payTo\";\n  let value: bigint;\n  try {\n    value = BigInt(a.value);\n  } catch {\n    return \"authorization.value is not an integer\";\n  }\n  if (value < BigInt(reqs.maxAmountRequired)) return \"insufficient_amount\";\n  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);\n  if (Number(a.validBefore) <= now + 5) return \"authorization expires too soon\";\n  if (Number(a.validAfter) > now) return \"authorization not yet valid\";\n  return null;\n}\n\nasync function handleForecast(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse, url: URL) {\n  const lat = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lat\"));\n  const lon = Number(url.searchParams.get(\"lon\"));\n  const hours = Math.min(168, Math.max(1, Number(url.searchParams.get(\"hours\") ?? 24)));\n\n  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lon) || Math.abs(lat) > 90 || Math.abs(lon) > 180) {\n    return send(res, 400, { error: \"lat and lon are required and must be valid coordinates\" });\n  }\n\n  const resourceUrl = `${cfg.origin}${url.pathname}${url.search}`;\n  const reqs = paymentRequirements(resourceUrl);\n  const header = req.headers[\"x-payment\"];\n\n  // No payment attached -> quote. This response is the entire onboarding flow:\n  // it tells a stranger the price, the token, the chain, the EIP-712 domain it\n  // must sign over, and the schema of what it will receive.\n  if (!header || typeof header !== \"string\") {\n    return send(res, 402, {\n      x402Version: 1,\n      error: \"X-PAYMENT header is required\",\n      accepts: [reqs],\n      extensions: { bazaar: BAZAAR_EXTENSION },\n    });\n  }\n\n  let payment: PaymentPayload;\n  try {\n    payment = decodePayment(header);\n  } catch {\n    return send(res, 402, {\n      x402Version: 1,\n      error: \"X-PAYMENT is not valid base64-encoded JSON\",\n      accepts: [reqs],\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Idempotency: a retried request with the same EIP-3009 nonce replays the\n  // stored response rather than attempting a second settlement.\n  const nonce = payment.payload?.authorization?.nonce;\n  const prior = nonce ? settled.get(nonce) : undefined;\n  if (prior) {\n    return send(res, 200, prior.body, {\n      \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE\": encodeSettlement(prior.settlement),\n      \"X-Receipt-Id\": prior.receiptId,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const bad = precheck(payment, reqs);\n  if (bad) {\n    return send(res, 402, { x402Version: 1, error: bad, accepts: [reqs] });\n  }\n\n  // 1. Verify before doing any work.\n  let verification: { isValid: boolean; invalidReason?: string; payer?: string };\n  try {\n    verification = await callFacilitator(\"/verify\", payment, reqs);\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[x402] verify failed:\", err);\n    return send(res, 503, { x402Version: 1, error: \"payment verification unavailable\" });\n  }\n  if (!verification.isValid) {\n    return send(res, 402, {\n      x402Version: 1,\n      error: verification.invalidReason ?? \"payment_invalid\",\n      accepts: [reqs],\n    });\n  }\n\n  // 2. Do the work. If this throws, we have not settled, so nobody is charged.\n  let forecast: Forecast;\n  try {\n    forecast = await getForecast(lat, lon, hours);\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[forecast] upstream failed:\", err);\n    return send(res, 502, { error: \"forecast upstream unavailable; you were not charged\" });\n  }\n\n  // 3. Settle. The facilitator broadcasts transferWithAuthorization and pays the\n  //    gas, which is what lets a caller holding only USDC pay us.\n  let settlement: SettlementResponse;\n  try {\n    settlement = await callFacilitator(\"/settle\", payment, reqs);\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"[x402] settle failed:\", err);\n    return send(res, 402, { x402Version: 1, error: \"settlement_failed\", accepts: [reqs] });\n  }\n  if (!settlement.success) {\n    return send(\n      res,\n      402,\n      { x402Version: 1, error: settlement.errorReason ?? \"settlement_failed\", accepts: [reqs] },\n      { \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE\": encodeSettlement(settlement) },\n    );\n  }\n\n  const body = JSON.stringify(forecast, null, 2);\n  const receipt = await buildReceipt({\n    payer: settlement.payer ?? verification.payer ?? payment.payload.authorization.from,\n    txHash: settlement.transaction,\n    requestUrl: resourceUrl,\n    responseBody: body,\n  });\n  receipts.set(receipt.receiptId, receipt);\n  if (nonce) settled.set(nonce, { body, receiptId: receipt.receiptId, settlement });\n\n  return send(res, 200, body, {\n    \"X-PAYMENT-RESPONSE\": encodeSettlement(settlement),\n    \"X-Receipt-Id\": receipt.receiptId,\n  });\n}\n\n/** Mirror a client-authored ERC-8004 feedback file. Content-addressed. */\nasync function handleFeedbackMirror(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse) {\n  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];\n  let size = 0;\n  for await (const chunk of req) {\n    size += chunk.length;\n    if (size > 64 * 1024) return send(res, 413, { error: \"feedback file too large\" });\n    chunks.push(chunk as Buffer);\n  }\n  const raw = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString(\"utf8\");\n  try {\n    JSON.parse(raw);\n  } catch {\n    return send(res, 400, { error: \"body must be a JSON feedback file\" });\n  }\n\n  const id = sha256(raw).slice(0, 32);\n  feedbackFiles.set(id, raw);\n  return send(res, 201, {\n    feedbackURI: `${cfg.origin}/feedback/${id}.json`,\n    // The value to pass as giveFeedback's feedbackHash. Verify it yourself\n    // before committing: we host this, so you should not trust us to host it\n    // honestly. IPFS is the censorship-resistant option.\n    feedbackHash: keccak256(toHex(raw)),\n    note: \"Verify feedbackHash against your own bytes before calling giveFeedback.\",\n  });\n}\n\n/** Convenience read of the onchain score.
